Safe input reader ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef safeGet = { varName, defaultVal ->\n   try {\n       if (binding.hasVariable(varName)) {\n           def val = binding.getVariable(varName)\n           return val != null ? val : defaultVal\n       }\n       return defaultVal\n   } catch (Exception e) {\n       return defaultVal\n   }\n}\n\n// ─── 0b.
